wet_ngs_count_agents = {
  set_variable = {
    wet_ngs_active_courts = 0
  }
  set_variable = {
    wet_ngs_surviving_courts = 0
  }
  if = {
    limit = {
      wet_ngs_agent_A13 = yes
    }
    add_to_variable = {
      wet_ngs_active_courts = 1
    }
  }
  if = {
    limit = {
      A13 = {
        exists = yes
        has_character = WET_A13_leader
        has_idea = wet_deathless_hosts
      }
    }
    add_to_variable = {
      wet_ngs_surviving_courts = 1
    }
  }
  if = {
    limit = {
      wet_ngs_agent_B16 = yes
    }
    add_to_variable = {
      wet_ngs_active_courts = 1
    }
  }
  if = {
    limit = {
      B16 = {
        exists = yes
        has_character = WET_B16_leader
        has_idea = wet_deathless_hosts
      }
    }
    add_to_variable = {
      wet_ngs_surviving_courts = 1
    }
  }
  if = {
    limit = {
      wet_ngs_agent_A20 = yes
    }
    add_to_variable = {
      wet_ngs_active_courts = 1
    }
  }
  if = {
    limit = {
      A20 = {
        exists = yes
        has_character = WET_A20_leader
        has_idea = wet_deathless_hosts
      }
    }
    add_to_variable = {
      wet_ngs_surviving_courts = 1
    }
  }
  if = {
    limit = {
      wet_ngs_agent_A68 = yes
    }
    add_to_variable = {
      wet_ngs_active_courts = 1
    }
  }
  if = {
    limit = {
      A68 = {
        exists = yes
        has_character = WET_A68_leader
        has_idea = wet_deathless_hosts
      }
    }
    add_to_variable = {
      wet_ngs_surviving_courts = 1
    }
  }
  if = {
    limit = {
      wet_ngs_agent_B27 = yes
    }
    add_to_variable = {
      wet_ngs_active_courts = 1
    }
  }
  if = {
    limit = {
      B27 = {
        exists = yes
        has_character = WET_B27_leader
        has_idea = wet_deathless_hosts
      }
    }
    add_to_variable = {
      wet_ngs_surviving_courts = 1
    }
  }
  if = {
    limit = {
      wet_ngs_agent_V01 = yes
    }
    add_to_variable = {
      wet_ngs_active_courts = 1
    }
  }
  if = {
    limit = {
      V01 = {
        exists = yes
        has_character = WET_V01_leader
        has_idea = wet_deathless_hosts
      }
    }
    add_to_variable = {
      wet_ngs_surviving_courts = 1
    }
  }
  if = {
    limit = {
      wet_ngs_agent_U18 = yes
    }
    add_to_variable = {
      wet_ngs_active_courts = 1
    }
  }
  if = {
    limit = {
      U18 = {
        exists = yes
        has_character = WET_U18_leader
        has_idea = wet_deathless_hosts
      }
    }
    add_to_variable = {
      wet_ngs_surviving_courts = 1
    }
  }
  if = {
    limit = {
      wet_ngs_agent_A74 = yes
    }
    add_to_variable = {
      wet_ngs_active_courts = 1
    }
  }
  if = {
    limit = {
      A74 = {
        exists = yes
        has_character = WET_A74_leader
        has_idea = wet_deathless_hosts
      }
    }
    add_to_variable = {
      wet_ngs_surviving_courts = 1
    }
  }
  set_variable = {
    wet_ngs_effective_agents = wet_ngs_active_courts
  }
  if = {
    limit = {
      check_variable = {
        wet_ngs_active_courts
        <
        3
      }
    }
    add_to_variable = {
      wet_ngs_effective_agents = wet_ngs_replacement_circles
    }
    clamp_variable = {
      var = wet_ngs_effective_agents
      max = 3
    }
  }
}
wet_ngs_send_covenant = {
  if = {
    limit = {
      wet_ngs_court_eligible = yes
      wet_ngs_named_agent_present = yes
      NOT = {
        has_country_flag = wet_ngs_withdrawn
      }
      NOT = {
        has_country_flag = wet_ngs_pledged
      }
      U24 = {
        exists = yes
      }
      NOT = {
        has_country_flag = wet_ngs_offer_covenant
      }
      check_variable = {
        wet_ngs_offer_covenant_cooldown
        <
        1
      }
      NOT = {
        has_country_flag = wet_ngs_offer_covenant_accepted
      }
      NOT = {
        has_country_flag = wet_ngs_offer_covenant_attempted
      }
    }
    set_country_flag = wet_ngs_offer_covenant_attempted
    set_country_flag = wet_ngs_offer_covenant
    set_variable = {
      wet_ngs_offer_covenant_days = 30
    }
    U24 = {
      if = {
        limit = {
          NOT = {
            is_in_array = {
              wet_ngs_active_recipients = PREV
            }
          }
        }
        add_to_array = {
          wet_ngs_active_recipients = PREV
        }
      }
    }
    country_event = {
      id = wet_nagashi.100
    }
  }
}
wet_ngs_send_research_exchange = {
  if = {
    limit = {
      wet_ngs_court_eligible = yes
      has_country_flag = wet_ngs_pledged
      U24 = {
        has_completed_focus = WET_NGS_M20
        wet_ngs_six_agents = yes
      }
      NOT = {
        has_country_flag = wet_ngs_offer_research_exchange
      }
      check_variable = {
        wet_ngs_offer_research_exchange_cooldown
        <
        1
      }
      NOT = {
        has_country_flag = wet_ngs_offer_research_exchange_accepted
      }
      OR = {
        NOT = {
          has_country_flag = wet_ngs_offer_research_exchange_attempted
        }
        U24 = {
          always = yes
        }
      }
    }
    if = {
      limit = {
        has_country_flag = wet_ngs_offer_research_exchange_attempted
      }
      U24 = {

      }
    }
    set_country_flag = wet_ngs_offer_research_exchange_attempted
    set_country_flag = wet_ngs_offer_research_exchange
    set_variable = {
      wet_ngs_offer_research_exchange_days = 30
    }
    U24 = {
      if = {
        limit = {
          NOT = {
            is_in_array = {
              wet_ngs_active_recipients = PREV
            }
          }
        }
        add_to_array = {
          wet_ngs_active_recipients = PREV
        }
      }
    }
    country_event = {
      id = wet_nagashi.102
    }
  }
}
wet_ngs_recover_A01 = {
  every_country = {
    limit = {
      NOT = {
        tag = U24
      }
      has_country_flag = wet_ngs_relic_A01
    }
    country_event = {
      id = wet_nagashi.103
    }
  }
  every_country = {
    limit = {
      has_country_flag = wet_ngs_relic_A01
    }
    clr_country_flag = wet_ngs_relic_A01
  }
  every_state = {
    limit = {
      has_state_flag = wet_ngs_relic_A01_site
    }
    clr_state_flag = wet_ngs_relic_A01_site
  }
  set_country_flag = wet_ngs_relic_A01
  capital_scope = {
    set_state_flag = wet_ngs_relic_A01_site
  }
  add_to_variable = {
    global.wet_ngs_relic_A01_generation = 1
  }
  if = {
    limit = {
      NOT = {
        has_country_flag = wet_ngs_relic_bonus_A01
      }
    }
    set_country_flag = wet_ngs_relic_bonus_A01
    add_to_variable = {
      wet_ngs_programme_research_speed_factor = 0.01
    }
  }
  wet_ngs_count_relics = yes
}

    }

  set_global_flag = wet_brt_staff_exists
  set_country_flag = wet_ngs_guaranteed_staff_recovery
  set_country_flag = wet_brt_staff_operation_succeeded
  set_variable = {
    wet_brt_staff_operation_generation = global.wet_brt_staff_generation
  }
  wet_brt_take_staff = yes
  clr_country_flag = wet_ngs_guaranteed_staff_recovery
  set_country_flag = wet_brt_staff_move_authorised
  set_variable = {
    wet_brt_staff_operation_generation = global.wet_brt_staff_generation
  }
  wet_brt_relocate_staff = yes
  if = {
    limit = {
      NOT = {
        has_country_flag = wet_ngs_relic_bonus_A11
      }
    }
    set_country_flag = wet_ngs_relic_bonus_A11
    add_to_variable = {

wet_ngs_recover_A14 = {
  every_country = {
    limit = {
      NOT = {
        tag = U24
      }
      has_country_flag = wet_skv_fellblade_custodian
    }
    country_event = {
      id = wet_nagashi.103
    }
  }
  every_country = {
    limit = {
      has_country_flag = wet_skv_fellblade_custodian
    }
    clr_country_flag = wet_skv_fellblade_custodian
  }
  every_state = {
    limit = {
      has_state_flag = wet_skv_fellblade_site
    }
    clr_state_flag = wet_skv_fellblade_site
  }
  set_country_flag = wet_skv_fellblade_custodian
  capital_scope = {
    set_state_flag = wet_skv_fellblade_site
  }
  add_to_variable = {
    global.wet_skv_fellblade_generation = 1
  }
  set_global_flag = wet_skv_fellblade_exists
  if = {
    limit = {
      NOT = {
        has_country_flag = wet_ngs_relic_bonus_A14
      }
    }
    set_country_flag = wet_ngs_relic_bonus_A14
    add_to_variable = {
      wet_ngs_programme_army_attack_factor = 0.02
    }
  }
  wet_ngs_count_relics = yes
}

wet_ngs_send_volkmar_handover = {
  if = {
    limit = {
      tag = A13
      exists = yes
      has_country_flag = wet_ngs_pledged
      has_country_flag = wet_rei_volkmar_custodian
      237 = {
        is_fully_controlled_by = A13
      }
      A80 = {
        has_country_flag = wet_rei_volkmar_captive
        has_character = WET_A80_leader
      }
      NOT = {
        has_global_flag = wet_ngs_volkmar_captive
      }
      NOT = {
        has_global_flag = wet_story_volkmar_dead
      }
      U24 = {
        capital_scope = {
          is_fully_controlled_by = U24
        }
      }
      NOT = {
        has_country_flag = wet_ngs_offer_volkmar_handover
      }
      check_variable = {
        wet_ngs_offer_volkmar_handover_cooldown
        <
        1
      }
      OR = {
        NOT = {
          has_country_flag = wet_ngs_offer_volkmar_handover_attempted
        }
        U24 = {
          always = yes
        }
      }
    }
    if = {
      limit = {
        has_country_flag = wet_ngs_offer_volkmar_handover_attempted
      }
      U24 = {

      }
    }
    set_country_flag = wet_ngs_offer_volkmar_handover_attempted
    set_country_flag = wet_ngs_offer_volkmar_handover
    set_variable = {
      wet_ngs_offer_volkmar_handover_days = 30
    }
    U24 = {
      if = {
        limit = {
          NOT = {
            is_in_array = {
              wet_ngs_active_recipients = PREV
            }
          }
        }
        add_to_array = {
          wet_ngs_active_recipients = PREV
        }
      }
    }
    country_event = {
      id = wet_nagashi.108
    }
  }
}
wet_ngs_release_volkmar = {
  if = {
    limit = {
      has_global_flag = wet_ngs_volkmar_captive
      NOT = {
        has_global_flag = wet_ngs_volkmar_dead
      }
    }
    every_country = {
      limit = {
        has_character = WET_A80_leader
      }
      WET_A80_leader = {
        set_nationality = A80
      }
    }
    every_country = {
      limit = {
        has_country_flag = wet_ngs_volkmar_custodian
      }
      clr_country_flag = wet_ngs_volkmar_custodian
    }
    every_state = {
      limit = {
        has_state_flag = wet_ngs_volkmar_prison
      }
      clr_state_flag = wet_ngs_volkmar_prison
    }
    clr_global_flag = wet_ngs_volkmar_captive
# WET ruler command: captivity clr_global_flag
hidden_effect = { every_country = { limit = { has_character = WET_A80_leader } WET_A80_leader = { wet_ruler_restore_command = yes } } }

    add_to_variable = {
      global.wet_ngs_volkmar_generation = 1
    }
    A80 = {
      country_event = {
        id = wet_nagashi.107
      }
    }
    A80 = {
      clr_country_flag = wet_rei_volkmar_captive
    }
    A13 = {
      clr_country_flag = wet_rei_volkmar_custodian
    }
    set_global_flag = wet_story_volkmar_rescued
  }
}
wet_ngs_take_volkmar = {
  if = {
    limit = {
      wet_ngs_can_seize_volkmar = yes
    }
    A80 = {
      if = {
        limit = {
          has_government = democratic
          has_country_leader = {
            character = WET_A80_leader
            ruling_only = yes
          }
          has_character = WET_A80_commander_marshal
        }
        add_country_leader_role = {
          character = WET_A80_commander_marshal
          promote_leader = yes
          country_leader = {
            ideology = wet_order_cult_of_sigmar
            expire = "2902.1.31.1"
            traits = {
              wet_ruler_a80_commander_marshal
            }
          }
        }
# WET ruler command: appointment
hidden_effect = { WET_A80_commander_marshal = { wet_ruler_enable_field_marshal = yes } }

      }
      remove_country_leader_role = {
        character = WET_A80_leader
        ideology = wet_order_cult_of_sigmar
      }
      WET_A80_leader = {
        set_nationality = U24
      }
      country_event = {
        id = wet_nagashi.106
      }
    }
    set_global_flag = wet_ngs_volkmar_captive
# WET ruler command: captivity set_global_flag
hidden_effect = { every_country = { limit = { has_character = WET_A80_leader } WET_A80_leader = { wet_ruler_suspend_command = yes } } }

    set_country_flag = wet_ngs_volkmar_custodian
    capital_scope = {
      set_state_flag = wet_ngs_volkmar_prison
    }
    add_to_variable = {
      global.wet_ngs_volkmar_generation = 1
    }
    A80 = {
      set_country_flag = wet_rei_volkmar_captive
# WET ruler command: custody wet_rei_volkmar_captive
hidden_effect = { every_country = { limit = { has_character = WET_A80_leader } WET_A80_leader = { wet_ruler_suspend_command = yes } } }

    }
    A13 = {
      clr_country_flag = wet_rei_volkmar_custodian
    }
    set_global_flag = wet_story_volkmar_captured
    wet_ngs_bonus_volkmar = yes
    wet_ngs_count_victims = yes
  }
}
wet_ngs_sacrifice_volkmar = {
  if = {
    limit = {
      wet_ngs_holds_volkmar = yes
      has_country_flag = wet_ngs_selected_volkmar
    }
    retire_character = WET_A80_leader
    every_country = {
      limit = {
        has_country_flag = wet_ngs_volkmar_custodian
      }
      clr_country_flag = wet_ngs_volkmar_custodian
    }
    every_state = {
      limit = {
        has_state_flag = wet_ngs_volkmar_prison
      }
      clr_state_flag = wet_ngs_volkmar_prison
    }
    clr_global_flag = wet_ngs_volkmar_captive
# WET ruler command: captivity clr_global_flag
hidden_effect = { every_country = { limit = { has_character = WET_A80_leader } WET_A80_leader = { wet_ruler_restore_command = yes } } }

    add_to_variable = {
      global.wet_ngs_volkmar_generation = 1
    }
    set_global_flag = wet_ngs_volkmar_dead
    add_to_variable = {
      wet_ngs_sacrificed_now = 1
    }
    A80 = {
      add_stability = -0.1
    }
    A80 = {
      clr_country_flag = wet_rei_volkmar_captive
    }
    set_global_flag = wet_story_volkmar_dead
    clr_country_flag = wet_ngs_selected_volkmar
  }
}

wet_ngs_finish_project = {
  set_country_flag = wet_ngs_project_output_earned
  if = {
    limit = {
      208 = {
        is_owned_by = U24
        is_fully_controlled_by = U24
      }
      wet_ngs_charge_ready = yes
    }
    if = {
      limit = {
        NOT = {
          has_country_flag = wet_ngs_vessel_made
        }
      }
      set_country_flag = wet_ngs_vessel_made
      208 = {
        set_state_flag = wet_ngs_vessel_site
      }
      if = {
        limit = {
          has_country_flag = wet_ngs_source_valaya
        }
        set_global_flag = wet_story_valaya_consumed
        every_country = {
          limit = {
            has_idea = wet_race_dwarf
          }
          add_opinion_modifier = {
            target = U24
            modifier = wet_ngs_valaya_drained
          }
        }
      }
    }
  }
}
wet_ngs_resurrect = {
  wet_ngs_reconcile_custody = yes
  if = {
    limit = {
      wet_ngs_ritual_ready = yes
    }
    wet_ngs_select_default_victims = yes
    wet_ngs_sacrifice_selected = yes
    if = {
      limit = {
        check_variable = {
          wet_ngs_sacrificed_now = 3
        }
      }
      set_country_flag = wet_ngs_restored
      set_global_flag = wet_story_nagash_resurrected
      set_character_name = {
        character = WET_U24_leader
        name = WET_NGS_Nagash
      }
      add_country_leader_role = {
        character = WET_U24_leader
        promote_leader = yes
        country_leader = {
          ideology = wet_undead_nagash_worship
          expire = "2902.1.31.1"
          traits = {
            wet_ruler_u24_leader
          }
        }
      }
# WET ruler command: appointment
hidden_effect = { WET_U24_leader = { wet_ruler_enable_field_marshal = yes } }

      add_ideas = wet_ngs_supreme_lord
      every_owned_state = {
        limit = {
          impassable = no
          is_fully_controlled_by = U24
        }
        set_variable = {
          wet_ngs_restoration_population = state_population_k
        }
        multiply_variable = {
          wet_ngs_restoration_population = 200
        }
        add_manpower = var:wet_ngs_restoration_population
      }
      if = {
        limit = {
          has_country_flag = wet_ngs_source_valaya
        }
        add_ideas = wet_ngs_source_valaya
      }
      else_if = {
        limit = {
          has_country_flag = wet_ngs_source_warpstone
        }
        add_ideas = wet_ngs_source_warpstone
      }
      else = {
        add_ideas = wet_ngs_source_souls
        for_each_scope_loop = {
          array = wet_ngs_project_donors
          if = {
            limit = {
              exists = yes
            }
            add_ideas = wet_ngs_source_souls
          }
        }
      }
      country_event = {
        id = wet_nagashi.132
      }
    }
  }

              wet_ngs_goal_queue_N07 = PREV
            }
          }
        }
        add_to_array = {
          wet_ngs_goal_queue_N07 = PREV
        }
      }
    }
  }
  else = {
    wet_ngs_issue_goal_N07 = yes
  }
}
wet_ngs_issue_goal_N08 = {
  clear_array = wet_ngs_goal_states
  if = {
    limit = {
      618 = {
        OR = {
          is_owned_by = PREV
          owner = {
            is_subject_of = PREV.PREV
          }
        }
      }
    }
    add_to_array = {
      wet_ngs_goal_states = 618
    }
  }
  if = {
    limit = {
      check_variable = {
        wet_ngs_goal_states^num
        >
        0
      }
      U24 = {
        OR = {
          is_ai = no
          is_neighbor_of = PREV
        }
      }
    }
    U24 = {
      create_wargoal = {
        type = take_state_focus
        target = PREV
        generator = PREV.wet_ngs_goal_states
        expire = 180
      }
      if = {
        limit = {
          is_ai = yes
        }
        set_country_flag = wet_ngs_goal_issued_today
      }
      log = "WET_NGS_CAMPAIGN_GOAL attacker=U24 target=[PREV.GetTag] focus=N08"
    }
  }
  clear_array = wet_ngs_goal_states
}
wet_ngs_receive_goal_N08 = {
  if = {

wet_ngs_issue_goal_E04 = {

    }
  }

    limit = {

        >
        0

wet_ngs_refresh_efforts = {
  for_each_scope_loop = {
    array = wet_ngs_bonus_enemies
    U24 = {
      remove_dynamic_modifier = {
        modifier = wet_ngs_south_effort
        scope = PREV
      }
      remove_dynamic_modifier = {
        modifier = wet_ngs_north_effort
        scope = PREV
      }
      remove_dynamic_modifier = {
        modifier = wet_ngs_plague_effort
        scope = PREV
      }
    }
  }
  clear_array = wet_ngs_bonus_enemies
  every_enemy_country = {
    U24 = {
      add_to_array = {
        wet_ngs_bonus_enemies = PREV
      }
    }
    if = {
      limit = {
        wet_ngs_south_enemy = yes
        U24 = {
          check_variable = {
            wet_ngs_effort_south_days
            >
            0
          }
        }
      }
      U24 = {
        add_dynamic_modifier = {
          modifier = wet_ngs_south_effort
          scope = PREV
        }
      }
    }
    if = {
      limit = {
        wet_ngs_everchosen_enemy = yes
        U24 = {
          check_variable = {
            wet_ngs_effort_north_days
            >
            0
          }
        }
      }
      U24 = {
        add_dynamic_modifier = {
          modifier = wet_ngs_north_effort
          scope = PREV
        }
      }
    }
    if = {
      limit = {
        wet_ngs_nurgle = yes
        U24 = {
          has_completed_focus = WET_NGS_E06
        }
      }
      U24 = {
        add_dynamic_modifier = {
          modifier = wet_ngs_plague_effort
          scope = PREV
        }
      }
    }
  }
}
wet_ngs_snapshot_battlefields = {
  every_enemy_country = {
    limit = {
      wet_ngs_everchosen_enemy = yes
    }
    every_owned_state = {
      limit = {
        NOT = {
          has_state_flag = wet_ngs_muster_baseline
        }
      }
      set_state_flag = wet_ngs_muster_baseline
      set_variable = {
        wet_ngs_muster_amount = state_population_k
      }
      multiply_variable = {
        wet_ngs_muster_amount = 20
      }
    }
  }
}

      wet_ngs_nehekhara_secured = yes

    if = {
      limit = {
        Z01 = {
          exists = yes
          has_character = WET_Z01_leader
        }
      }
      set_global_flag = wet_ngs_settra_offered
      Z01 = {
        country_event = {
          id = wet_nagashi.135
        }
      }
    }
    else_if = {
      limit = {
        has_character = WET_Z01_leader

      }
      set_global_flag = wet_ngs_settra_offered
      country_event = {
        id = wet_nagashi.133
      }
    }
  }
}

wet_ngs_try_campaign_goals = {
  if = {
    limit = {
      is_ai = yes
      wet_ngs_ai_goal_slot_free = yes
    }
    clear_array = wet_ngs_processed_goals
    for_each_scope_loop = {
      array = wet_ngs_goal_queue_H03
      if = {
        limit = {
          OR = {
            exists = no
            NOT = {
              wet_ngs_target_H03 = yes
            }
          }
        }
        U24 = {
          add_to_array = {
            wet_ngs_processed_goals = PREV
          }
        }
      }
      else_if = {
        limit = {
          wet_ngs_goal_legal = yes
          U24 = {
            wet_ngs_ai_goal_slot_free = yes
          }
        }
        wet_ngs_issue_goal_H03 = yes
        if = {
          limit = {
            U24 = {
              has_country_flag = wet_ngs_goal_issued_today
            }
          }
          U24 = {
            add_to_array = {
              wet_ngs_processed_goals = PREV
            }
          }
        }
      }
    }
    for_each_scope_loop = {
      array = wet_ngs_processed_goals
      U24 = {
        remove_from_array = {
          wet_ngs_goal_queue_H03 = PREV
        }
      }
    }
    clear_array = wet_ngs_processed_goals
    clear_array = wet_ngs_processed_goals
    for_each_scope_loop = {
      array = wet_ngs_goal_queue_H06
      if = {
        limit = {
          OR = {
            exists = no
            NOT = {
              wet_ngs_target_H06 = yes
            }
          }
        }
        U24 = {
          add_to_array = {
            wet_ngs_processed_goals = PREV
          }
        }
      }
      else_if = {
        limit = {
          wet_ngs_goal_legal = yes
          U24 = {
            wet_ngs_ai_goal_slot_free = yes
          }
        }
        wet_ngs_issue_goal_H06 = yes
        if = {
          limit = {
            U24 = {
              has_country_flag = wet_ngs_goal_issued_today
            }
          }
          U24 = {
            add_to_array = {
              wet_ngs_processed_goals = PREV
            }
          }
        }
      }
    }
    for_each_scope_loop = {
      array = wet_ngs_processed_goals
      U24 = {
        remove_from_array = {
          wet_ngs_goal_queue_H06 = PREV
        }
      }
    }
    clear_array = wet_ngs_processed_goals
    clear_array = wet_ngs_processed_goals
    for_each_scope_loop = {
      array = wet_ngs_goal_queue_H08
      if = {
        limit = {
          OR = {
            exists = no
            NOT = {
              wet_ngs_target_H08 = yes
            }
          }
        }
        U24 = {
          add_to_array = {
            wet_ngs_processed_goals = PREV
          }
        }
      }
      else_if = {
        limit = {
          wet_ngs_goal_legal = yes
          U24 = {
            wet_ngs_ai_goal_slot_free = yes
          }
        }
        wet_ngs_issue_goal_H08 = yes
        if = {
          limit = {
            U24 = {
              has_country_flag = wet_ngs_goal_issued_today
            }
          }

          wet_ngs_release_mazdamundi = yes
        }
        else = {
          every_country = {
            limit = {
              has_country_flag = wet_ngs_mazdamundi_custodian
            }
            clr_country_flag = wet_ngs_mazdamundi_custodian
            if = {
              limit = {
                has_character = WET_V04_leader
              }
              WET_V04_leader = {
                set_nationality = PREV.PREV
              }
            }
          }
          set_country_flag = wet_ngs_mazdamundi_custodian
          add_to_variable = {
            global.wet_ngs_mazdamundi_generation = 1
          }
        }
      }
    }
    if = {
      limit = {
        NOT = {
          any_country = {
            has_character = WET_V04_leader
          }
        }
      }
      every_country = {
        limit = {
          has_country_flag = wet_ngs_mazdamundi_custodian
        }
        clr_country_flag = wet_ngs_mazdamundi_custodian
      }
      every_state = {
        limit = {
          has_state_flag = wet_ngs_mazdamundi_prison
        }
        clr_state_flag = wet_ngs_mazdamundi_prison
      }
      clr_global_flag = wet_ngs_mazdamundi_captive
# WET ruler command: captivity clr_global_flag
hidden_effect = { every_country = { limit = { has_character = WET_V04_leader } WET_V04_leader = { wet_ruler_restore_command = yes } } }

      add_to_variable = {
        global.wet_ngs_mazdamundi_generation = 1
      }
      set_global_flag = wet_ngs_mazdamundi_dead
    }
  }
  every_controlled_state = {
    limit = {
      is_fully_controlled_by = U24
      owner = {
        wet_ngs_everchosen_enemy = yes
      }
      NOT = {
        has_state_flag = wet_ngs_muster_recorded
      }
    }
    set_state_flag = wet_ngs_muster_recorded
  }
  H06 = {
    wet_ult_refresh_custody = yes
  }
  wet_ngs_count_relics = yes
  wet_ngs_count_victims = yes
}

wet_focus_actions_nagashi = {
  if = {
    limit = {
      has_country_flag = wet_ngs_abduct_volkmar_focus_running
    }
    if = {
      limit = {
        NOT = {
          AND = {
            wet_ngs_can_seize_volkmar = yes
          }
        }
      }
      clr_country_flag = wet_ngs_abduct_volkmar_focus_running
      set_variable = {
        wet_ngs_abduct_volkmar_focus_days = 0
      }
      if = {
        limit = {
          has_country_flag = wet_ngs_abduct_volkmar_active
        }
        clr_country_flag = wet_ngs_abduct_volkmar_active
        add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
          type = support_equipment
          amount = 50
        }
      }
    }
    else = {
      subtract_from_variable = {
        wet_ngs_abduct_volkmar_focus_days = 1
      }
      if = {
        limit = {
          check_variable = {
            wet_ngs_abduct_volkmar_focus_days
            <
            1
          }
        }
        clr_country_flag = wet_ngs_abduct_volkmar_focus_running
        set_variable = {
          wet_ngs_abduct_volkmar_focus_days = 0
        }
        if = {
          limit = {
            has_country_flag = wet_ngs_abduct_volkmar_active
          }
          clr_country_flag = wet_ngs_abduct_volkmar_active
          if = {
            limit = {
              wet_ngs_can_seize_volkmar = yes
            }
            random_list = {
              85 = {
                modifier = {
                  add = -35
                  A80 = {
                    has_country_flag = wet_ngs_guard_volkmar
                  }
                }
                wet_ngs_take_volkmar = yes
              }
              15 = {
                modifier = {
                  add = 35
                  A80 = {
                    has_country_flag = wet_ngs_guard_volkmar
                  }
                }
                add_to_variable = {
                  wet_ngs_failed_volkmar = 1
                }
              }
            }
          }
          else = {
            add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
              type = support_equipment
              amount = 50
            }
          }
        }
      }
    }
  }
  else = {
    if = {
      limit = {
        has_country_flag = wet_ngs_abduct_volkmar_focus_pending
      }
      if = {
        limit = {
          wet_ngs_holds_volkmar = yes
        }
        clr_country_flag = wet_ngs_abduct_volkmar_focus_pending
      }
      else_if = {
        limit = {
          has_completed_focus = WET_NGS_V01
          has_equipment = {
            support_equipment
            >
            99
          }
          wet_ngs_can_seize_volkmar = yes
          always = yes
        }
        clr_country_flag = wet_ngs_abduct_volkmar_focus_pending
        set_country_flag = wet_ngs_abduct_volkmar_focus_running
        set_variable = {
          wet_ngs_abduct_volkmar_focus_days = 60

          }
        }
      }
    }
  }
  else = {
    if = {
      limit = {
        has_country_flag = wet_ngs_raise_pyramid_focus_pending
      }
      if = {
        limit = {
          always = no
        }
        clr_country_flag = wet_ngs_raise_pyramid_focus_pending
      }
      else_if = {
        limit = {
          has_completed_focus = WET_NGS_N13
          607 = {
            is_owned_by = U24
            is_fully_controlled_by = U24
          }
          has_equipment = {
            support_equipment
            >
            999
          }
          wet_skv_nagash_leader = yes
          NOT = {
            has_global_flag = wet_skv_pyramid_exists
          }
          NOT = {
            has_global_flag = wet_skv_pyramid_build_pending
          }
          always = yes
        }
        clr_country_flag = wet_ngs_raise_pyramid_focus_pending
        set_country_flag = wet_ngs_raise_pyramid_focus_running
        set_variable = {
          wet_ngs_raise_pyramid_focus_days = 360
        }
        set_country_flag = wet_ngs_raise_pyramid_active
        set_global_flag = wet_skv_pyramid_build_pending
        set_country_flag = wet_skv_pyramid_building
        add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
          type = support_equipment
          amount = -1000
        }
        607 = {
          set_state_flag = wet_skv_pyramid_construction
        }
      }
    }
  }
  if = {
    limit = {
      has_country_flag = wet_ngs_restoration_without_dlc_focus_running
    }
    if = {
      limit = {
        NOT = {
          AND = {
            208 = {
              is_owned_by = U24
              is_fully_controlled_by = U24
            }
            has_country_flag = wet_ngs_project_funded
            wet_ngs_charge_ready = yes
            NOT = {
              has_country_flag = wet_ngs_vessel_made
            }
            NOT = {
              has_dlc = "Gotterdammerung"
            }
          }
        }
      }
      clr_country_flag = wet_ngs_restoration_without_dlc_focus_running
      set_variable = {
        wet_ngs_restoration_without_dlc_focus_days = 0
      }
      if = {
        limit = {
          has_country_flag = wet_ngs_restoration_without_dlc_active
        }
        clr_country_flag = wet_ngs_restoration_without_dlc_active
      }
    }
    else = {
      subtract_from_variable = {
        wet_ngs_restoration_without_dlc_focus_days = 1
      }
      if = {
        limit = {
          check_variable = {
            wet_ngs_restoration_without_dlc_focus_days
            <
            1
          }
        }
        clr_country_flag = wet_ngs_restoration_without_dlc_focus_running
        set_variable = {
          wet_ngs_restoration_without_dlc_focus_days = 0
        }
        if = {
          limit = {
            has_country_flag = wet_ngs_restoration_without_dlc_active
          }
          clr_country_flag = wet_ngs_restoration_without_dlc_active
          if = {
            limit = {
              208 = {
                is_owned_by = U24
                is_fully_controlled_by = U24
              }
              has_country_flag = wet_ngs_project_funded
              wet_ngs_charge_ready = yes
              NOT = {
                has_country_flag = wet_ngs_vessel_made
              }
              NOT = {
                has_dlc = "Gotterdammerung"
              }
            }
            wet_ngs_finish_project = yes
            set_country_flag = wet_ngs_restoration_without_dlc_done
          }
          else = {

          }
        }
      }
    }
  }
  else = {
    if = {
      limit = {
        has_country_flag = wet_ngs_restoration_without_dlc_focus_pending
      }
      if = {
        limit = {
          always = no
        }
        clr_country_flag = wet_ngs_restoration_without_dlc_focus_pending
      }
      else_if = {
        limit = {
          has_completed_focus = WET_NGS_P10
          NOT = {
            has_country_flag = wet_ngs_restoration_without_dlc_done
          }
          208 = {
            is_owned_by = U24
            is_fully_controlled_by = U24
          }
          has_country_flag = wet_ngs_project_funded
          wet_ngs_charge_ready = yes
          NOT = {
            has_country_flag = wet_ngs_vessel_made
          }
          NOT = {
            has_dlc = "Gotterdammerung"
          }
        }
        clr_country_flag = wet_ngs_restoration_without_dlc_focus_pending
        set_country_flag = wet_ngs_restoration_without_dlc_focus_running
        set_variable = {

        }

              }
              718 = {
                is_owned_by = PREV
                is_fully_controlled_by = PREV
              }

                    }

                }
              }
            }

          clr_country_flag = wet_ngs_abduct_alarielle_focus_pending
        }
        else_if = {
          limit = {
            has_completed_focus = WET_NGS_V05
            has_equipment = {
              support_equipment
              >
              99
            }
            wet_ngs_can_seize_alarielle = yes
            always = yes
          }
          clr_country_flag = wet_ngs_abduct_alarielle_focus_pending
          set_country_flag = wet_ngs_abduct_alarielle_focus_running
          set_variable = {
            wet_ngs_abduct_alarielle_focus_days = 60
          }
          set_country_flag = wet_ngs_abduct_alarielle_active
          add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
            type = support_equipment
            amount = -100
          }
          H06 = {
            country_event = {
              id = wet_nagashi.122
            }
          }
        }
      }
    }
    wet_ngs_reconcile_benefits = yes
  }
}
wet_ngs_award_V06 = {
  if = {
    limit = {
      tag = U24
      has_country_flag = wet_ngs_earned_V06
      NOT = {
        has_country_flag = wet_ngs_reward_V06
      }
      has_completed_focus = WET_NGS_A15
      OR = {
        V04 = {
          exists = yes
          has_character = WET_V04_leader
        }
        wet_ngs_holds_mazdamundi = yes
      }
      NOT = {
        has_global_flag = wet_ngs_mazdamundi_dead
      }
    }
    set_country_flag = wet_ngs_reward_V06
    set_country_flag = wet_ngs_kidnap_mazdamundi_open
    if = {
      limit = {
        always = yes
      }
      set_country_flag = wet_ngs_abduct_mazdamundi_focus_pending
      if = {
        limit = {
          has_country_flag = wet_ngs_abduct_mazdamundi_focus_pending
        }
        if = {
          limit = {
            wet_ngs_holds_mazdamundi = yes
          }
          clr_country_flag = wet_ngs_abduct_mazdamundi_focus_pending
        }
        else_if = {
          limit = {
            has_completed_focus = WET_NGS_V06
            has_equipment = {
              support_equipment
              >
              99
            }
            wet_ngs_can_seize_mazdamundi = yes
            always = yes
          }
          clr_country_flag = wet_ngs_abduct_mazdamundi_focus_pending
          set_country_flag = wet_ngs_abduct_mazdamundi_focus_running
          set_variable = {
            wet_ngs_abduct_mazdamundi_focus_days = 60
          }
          set_country_flag = wet_ngs_abduct_mazdamundi_active
          add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
            type = support_equipment
            amount = -100
          }
          V04 = {
            country_event = {
              id = wet_nagashi.126
            }
          }
        }
      }
    }
    wet_ngs_reconcile_benefits = yes
  }
}
